- **1.36.0** (2026-09-13): Phase CK-41 — **the publication gate: a default that inherits, and the first photograph that goes live** (the first of three phases implementing `decisions/2026-09-09-consent-gate-defaults.md` §10, amended to **2.0.0** in this phase: the host's account kind becomes a backstop, the `SEASON` clause is deleted, and the group a gathering belongs to is the discriminator; **backend only** — no frontend file, because CK-38 wrote the `live` copy before anything could reach it; **migration 0019**; the verifier at **0019/150**). New **`app/services/publication.py`** — the resolver, no database, no network, no logging: **one ladder readable as a list** — (1) the gathering's own setting (`requires_approval`, the host's choice; producer CK-43), (2) the home group's default (always None today; no `groups` column added — a column with no producer and no reach smuggles a feature in), **the ORGANIZATION-host backstop beneath the two rungs a person sets and above the two that are ours**, (3) the type's template (`capability_profiles.requires_approval`, reachable only through a home group — record §10.1 resolved as this rung), (4) the join shape, the only rung with a producer (`PRIVATE` opens, `PUBLIC` gates; `PUBLIC`'s producer is the share link, unbuilt); every answer carries its **source** so CK-43's accuracy statement needs no re-derivation; `resolve_gathering` is the one wiring point for today's facts. **Migration 0019**: `requires_approval` DROP NOT NULL and the default dropped — NULL means inherit; every existing row set to NULL (undecided — every `true` was CK-16's interim; the docstring says why the correction never recurs); every `ready`+`pending` photograph in a gathering that resolves open moved to `live`, once (the ladder in SQL for that instant — a LEFT JOIN to `accounts`, a hostless gathering resolving open at the join shape); the downgrade writes `false` over the NULLs and does not reverse the publication; **up/down/up verified with real subjects in the test DB** (a person-hosted ready+pending row → live, an org-hosted one stays pending, failed/uploaded/removed untouched; the downgrade restores `NO|false`). **`api/gatherings.py`**: the hard-coded `requires_approval=True` and its comment deleted — **create sets NOTHING on the column** (decision 22's discipline inverted on purpose, commented against being "fixed" back); every body carries `requires_approval` as the **effective** value resolved through the ladder (the list's one statement gaining an outer join to `accounts` for the host's kind — the CK-20 statement-count pin holds unedited) beside `requires_approval_override`, the host's own (null = inherited); **readable and not writable** — `PATCH` keeps its three-field surface, both fields draw the unknown-field 422 (CK-42's review surface ships before CK-43's override: an override that could turn review on before anything can approve would strand a gathering's photographs). **`services/ingest.py` step 4**: the gathering's setting and the host's kind read as the transaction's first statement, the gate resolved there — **at publish time, never snapshotted at intent** — and `pending → live` written in the SAME guarded statement as `ready` where open, only over `pending` (a takedown is never undone by the worker); no dead-letter path touches it; **`_visible_media` untouched**, its `live` branch getting its first subject. `worker.py` logs the state on its own line after the `ready` line. `verify_schema.py` → head 0019, **150** (3 new — `requires_approval` nullable: **an addition, not the edit the kickoff expected**, the verifier having never asserted the column's NOT NULL; no server default; every `live` media row is `ready` — each negative-tested, exit 1, restored; the "no ready+pending in an open gathering" assertion deliberately not added — CK-43 can legitimately break it, so it is check (dy)). **347 backend tests** (16 new: `tests/test_publication.py` 10; `test_worker.py` +5 — live in an open gathering and pending in a gated one read back in the CK-36 style, the org backstop at publish, a failed row pending, the gate read at claim not confirm in both directions, `live` inside the ready transaction observed from a second session; `test_gatherings.py` +1 — an org-hosted gathering constructed directly reads gated through the wiring). **Prior tests edited, four**: `test_gatherings.py`'s create pin (CK-16's `true` → NULL, the body's effective `false` and `null` override) and its PATCH-surface pin (`is True` → `is None`; the two gate fields added to the refused list), `test_invitations.py`'s one `is True` assertion (→ `is None`), `test_worker.py`'s crux pin (`pending` → `live`: the fixture gathering resolves open). Dev-deploy: 0018 → 0019 through the pre-deploy step; every gathering NULL; **(dz) — peter reading Steven's photographs, `live`**; a fresh upload reaching `ready`+`live` within a poll with the CK-38 `live` line rendering for the first time; the verifier at 0019/150 (WORKING-ON-NOW checks (dx)–(eb)). Report: `D:\projects\temp\CK-41-report.md`. *(Phase labels in this entry as written: the review surface CK-42 and the override CK-43 became CK-43 and CK-44 when the fourth doc audit took CK-42 — annotated at 1.36.1, not rewritten.)*
- **1.35.0** (2026-09-10): Phase CK-40 — **naming a photograph: the caption editor, tags, search, and the filename that finally arrives** (the frontend half of CK-39 — the CK-16/CK-17 split; **frontend only**: no backend file changed, no migration, head stays 0018, the verifier at 147 not re-run). **The CK-38 finding closed in full**: the intent now sends each file's name **trimmed** (`lib/media.ts::intentItem`) and the row is called by **its caption, then its filename, then "Photo"** (`mediaName`) from the list body — it survives a reload, and a `failed` row says which photograph failed; every pre-CK-39 row reads "Photo" permanently, correctly. **`size_bytes` is the `File`'s and nothing else** — the constraint CK-39's (dp) earned: the presigned PUT signs `Content-Length`, which fetch forbids the client to set, so a declared size that disagrees with the blob is refused on the signature and reads as an R2 credentials failure; any client-side resize or strip invalidates a size declared before it. New `MediaWordsEditor` in `components/GatheringMedia.tsx`, **on the caller's own rows only** (the server's uploader-not-host rule — no affordance that cannot succeed) **and never on a failed row** (§6.5's one-affordance pin stands): the caption under the CK-22 clearing rule (`captionPatch` — blank → explicit `null`, never `""`; already-empty omitted; whitespace-only as typed so the server's 422 renders); **the tag list REPLACES the set, so every save carries the whole list** (`tagsPatch`; pinned — one tag gains a second and ends with two, a removal is the set minus one; a partial list would be silent data loss); a duplicate (`Tommy`/`tommy` included) goes to the server and its 422 lands on the repeated entry; tags render as text chips, never a link (pinned with an address-shaped tag). **Search**: a box setting `q` after a 300 ms debounce (`SEARCH_DEBOUNCE_MS`), **no `q` at all for an empty or whitespace-only box** (`mediaListPath`), the server narrowing inside `_visible_media` and nothing cached across viewers; the filtered state legible ("Showing N photos matching …", **Show all photos**), an empty result a state ("No photos match …", the same way back — never "No photos yet"); adding photos leaves the filtered view; per gathering. `declaredContentType` trims the name before reading its extension (a trailing space would have declared the generic type for a good HEIC — found by the new pin). Three CK-38 pins reworded from "no button" to "no Try again" (an own row now carries its edit control); `routes/media.test.tsx`'s fixture gains the CK-39 fields and its stubs echo the filename. **99 frontend tests** (13 new: `lib/media-words.test.ts` 7, `routes/media-words.test.tsx` 6); backend untouched, 331 green; build + lint clean; `App.css` gains the chip, editor and search rules. Dev-deploy: checks (dr)–(dw) — the finding closed through the picker, the three CK-39 subjects on gathering `5fa6438e-…`, the tag test read from psql, the caption round trip with NULL never `""`, the three searches. Report: `D:\projects\temp\CK-40-report.md`.
- **1.34.0** (2026-09-10): Phase CK-39 — **words on a photograph: the filename, a caption, tags, and finding things by them** (the phase after the media arc; **backend only** — the caption editor, the tag entry and the search box are CK-40, the CK-16/CK-17 split; **migration 0018**; the verifier at **0018/147**, 17 new, every one negative-tested). **The finding it closes**: CK-38's (di) showed a row reading *"Photo — added by You"* after a reload because `media` had no filename column — an absence, not a choice — and on a `failed` row that meant "Try again" was re-uploading blind. **`media.filename`** (nullable; an optional per-item field on the intent — trimmed, capped at 255, a blank refused, NULL the one representation of "no name"; **nothing backfilled** — the five deployed rows read NULL forever, correctly) rides every media body and is **a display string and nothing else**: it never reaches a storage key (`quarantine_key`/`published_key` are pure functions of the row id — a traversal-shaped name is stored as text with the key still `uploads/<id>`, pinned), a path, or a header, and nothing relies on its extension. **`media.caption`** and **`media_tags`** (`MediaTag`: `media_id` FK **`ON DELETE CASCADE`**, `tag`, `added_by_person_id` FK → people with no delete rule — provenance, never a subject — `created_at`; **`UNIQUE (media_id, tag)`**) — **a table, not an array column, with two columns it must never grow, verifier-asserted absent: no `gathering_id`** (the join answers it; a second representation is the CK-13 defect) **and no `person_id` for a tagged person, not even nullable** (a person tag is a claim about someone else; it gets its own record and table — `decisions/2026-09-10-captions-tags-and-finding-a-photograph.md` §3). **New `PATCH /media/{id}` — the uploader, and NOT the host** (§7: a host may remove a photograph; re-captioning someone else's is a different power; the edit follows `_visible_media` and then narrows, 404 byte-identical to a missing id for everyone else, the host on a `pending` row they can see included): JSON Merge Patch on `caption` (explicit null clears — NULL, never `""`; blank a field-level 422; absent leaves alone; empty patch = no fields provided) and **`tags`, a list that REPLACES the set — never appends** (pinned; `[]` clears; `null` refused; ≤ 20 tags of ≤ 50 chars, trimmed, blank refused on the entry; **the same tag twice refused on the entry that repeats it, and `Tommy`/`tommy` are one tag** — case preserved, equality case-insensitive). **`q` on `GET /gatherings/{id}/media`** — a case-insensitive substring over caption, tag text and filename, the term literal (`%`/`_` escaped), ≤ 200 chars; **the naive query with NO index, decided** (revisit: the first gathering holding a few hundred photographs); **inside the audience filter, never beside it** — ANDed with `_visible_media` in one `WHERE`, pinned by the leak test (a keeper and an accepted invitee searching a `pending` photograph's caption, tag and filename get the empty list a term matching nothing returns; the host finds it; a keeper finds a `live` one); per gathering; the list still ONE statement with the tags as a correlated array subquery (pinned). Intent/confirm bodies carry `filename` and `caption` and never `tags` (a body that did not load them must not claim `[]`). The router logs no word (pinned with the root at DEBUG); `q` rides the query string, so an access log's request line carries it as it carries every path — stated, not pinned away. Not added, with triggers recorded: `media.updated_at` (two writers on the row), person tags, gathering-scoped tags, cross-gathering search, an index. `verify_schema.py` → head **0018, 147 assertions** (17 new: the two columns nullable; the table and its three NOT NULLs; the four absent columns; the FK's target and CASCADE; the UNIQUE; no blank filename/caption, no blank tag, no case-twin tags, ≤ 20 tags — every one negative-tested: scratch rows plus the cascade replaced and the UNIQUE dropped → 6 FAILs; a temporary downgrade → 9 FAILs; exit 1 each, restored to 147/0). **331 backend tests** (10 new, `tests/test_media_words.py`; prior tests unedited); `alembic check` clean; 0018 up/down/up verified in psql; frontend untouched. Dev-deploy: the deploy carries 0018 through the pre-deploy step; a caption and two tags on the two `ready` photographs, three searches, the caption cleared with an explicit null and read back as NULL, a new upload carrying its file name across a reload, the verifier at 0018/147 (WORKING-ON-NOW checks (dn)–(dq)). Report: `D:\projects\temp\CK-39-report.md`.
- **1.33.0** (2026-09-09): Phase CK-38 — **the upload surface: a photograph you can actually add, and four states you can actually see** (seventh phase of the media arc; **frontend only** — no backend file changed, no migration, head stays 0017, the verifier at 130; **the first time the arc is usable without a REST client**). New **`components/GatheringMedia.tsx`** (the Photos section of `/gatherings/:id` — for everyone in the read audience, **collapsed by default** so the detail page stays one request; the server's audience rule decides who sees anything, `isHost` only chooses the wording) and **`lib/media.ts`** (the API types, the one state-message function, and the two calls that spend a presigned URL). **The upload control**: intent → PUT → confirm per file, the batch endpoint used as a batch. Three details earned by manual runs and not re-derived: **`file.type` is empty for HEIC in the browser** (CK-36's (da)), so `declaredContentType` falls back to the extension — the declared type is signed into the presigned PUT and a wrong one reads as an R2 permissions failure; an unnameable type goes out as the generic binary type so the **server** refuses it in its own words (the client never pre-empts the allowlist; the picker's `accept` mirrors it and is never the authority — a video chosen anyway draws the server's 422 inline against the file it names, the batch refused whole, "Nothing was uploaded"); **Content-Length is never set** (forbidden in fetch — the browser sets it from the body, which is what makes the signature match); the PUT carries no Authorization header (R2 is not our API). **The presigned URL never leaves the network call**: the intent response is a local of the upload handler, spent on the PUTs and gone — state holds media ids and file names; **a layer is fetched into a `blob:` object URL** (`fetchLayerObjectUrl`: mint through our API, one fetch, `URL.createObjectURL`, revoked on unmount) so what an `<img>` and React hold carries no credential — pinned on the PUT, the thumbnail and the web layer: the URL appears in exactly one fetch call and never in the DOM. **The four states, shown honestly** — `mediaStateMessage` is **a function of `status` AND `publication_state`, never a constant**: `pending_upload` on its way / `uploaded` waiting to be prepared / `processing` being prepared, usually within a minute, longer for a big batch (record §6.3's own words) / `ready`+`pending` **who can see it** — "Only you and the host can see this" (collapsing to "Only you" when the uploader is the host; "Only grandma and you" for the host) / **`ready`+`live` "Everyone in this gathering can see it" — written now, nothing reaches it yet**, so when the publication phase implements `decisions/2026-09-09-consent-gate-defaults.md` §1 (a private, person-owned gathering does not wait for anyone — every gathering today) these rows change message with no copy rewrite / `ready`+`removed` the bin's line / `failed` "We couldn't process this photo" with **exactly one affordance, Try again, which re-uploads from the device** (record §6.5 — never a re-queue; pinned that a ready row offers none, which the first run of the test caught as a real bug). **No copy names a reviewer, an approval, or calls a pending photograph shared** — pinned at the source (comments stripped, then every string scanned); CK-37's "waiting for the host, or nothing" resolved to *nothing*, per consent-gate-defaults §9 — for a family gathering nobody will ever review it. A PUT that fails from this device overrides the server's "on its way" with "This upload didn't finish" — the device knows what the server cannot. **Polling**: while any row is non-terminal the list re-reads every 3 s (`POLL_INTERVAL_MS`; CK-36 measured ~4.7 s claim-to-ready) and stops the moment everything is `ready` or `failed`; a page with nothing in flight makes no request at all; a failed load stops polling and offers a retry. `thumbnail` in the list, `web` when opened, **`archival` never requested** (pinned). The upload hint states the real order (record §3): uploaded, then prepared, location data removed as part of that. **One dependency verifiable only on the deploy**: the blob read needs the published bucket's CORS to allow GET from the frontend origin (the quarantine bucket's has held for PUTs since CK-34; nothing records the published bucket's) — if the URL endpoint answers 200 and the thumbnail says "Couldn't show this photo just now" with a CORS error in the console, that is bucket configuration, an out-of-band fix, not a code bug (WORKING-ON-NOW check (dj)). **86 frontend tests** (17 new: `lib/media.test.ts` 8, `routes/media.test.tsx` 9; prior tests unedited — the section is collapsed, so no existing detail test sees a media request); backend untouched, 321 green; build + lint clean. Dev-deploy: the first upload through the UI as a person would, two claim-to-ready figures from the worker's `ready` line (the first production has ever emitted), a video refused through the picker, the honesty check in two parts (WORKING-ON-NOW checks (dh)–(dm)). Report: `D:\projects\temp\CK-38-report.md`.
- **1.32.0** (2026-09-09): Phase CK-37 — **reading a photograph back: who may see one that nobody has approved** (sixth phase of the media arc; **backend only**; **no migration** — head stays 0017, `alembic check` clean; `verify_schema.py` stays at 130, deliberately). **Step 0 — the audience, decided and recorded before the endpoint** (`decisions/2026-09-09-who-may-see-an-unapproved-photograph.md`, docs root): every media row is `pending`, the host's approval surface is unbuilt, and letting the gathering's read audience see everything "because the gate isn't built yet" would show unapproved photographs of children to an entire gathering. So, per `publication_state`: **`live`** → the read audience (`_gathering_for_read`: host OR keeper OR accepted invitee — built and pinned though nothing is live, so the publication phase inherits it); **`pending` → the uploader and the host, and nobody else** (a keeper is not an approver; an invitation is visibility of the gathering, never of unreviewed media); **`removed`** → the uploader alone for `REMOVED_BIN` (30 days) after `removed_at`, the host excluded — the bin's read window defined before removal exists. **NOT interim.** **404-not-403 before any other refusal**, and ONE SQL criterion — `api/media.py::_visible_media`, over `media` joined to its gathering with the audience as EXISTS subqueries — that both endpoints apply; the list requires the gathering's read audience first (a stranger draws the gathering 404) and then filters, so a keeper who uploaded nothing gets `200` and `[]`. **Two endpoints**: `GET /gatherings/{id}/media` (every rung with its `status` and `publication_state`, the uploader's display name — never an email or person id — `is_own`, the label, the bin clock; **no URL**, no `last_error`) and `GET /media/{id}/url?layer=web|thumbnail` (one presigned GET through the SERVE credential — `storage.presign_read`, a per-process `ServeClient` — for the derivative row's own `storage_key`; **422 for `archival`**, the print master on Infrequent Access, never served; **409 `not_ready` carrying the rung** for anything but `ready`; the order 404 → 422 → 409 so nothing confirms a row exists or hints at its state). **No URLs in the list, by design**: minting on demand keeps a bearer credential from existing for any object nobody is about to fetch; the cost (a grid of N thumbnails is N requests, each one indexed lookup and one local HMAC) is recorded with its trigger (CK-38 finds it hurts → a batch form on the same criterion). **§9 at volume — never logged**: the module still has no logger; pinned with the root logger at DEBUG across the success path and the 404/409/422 paths — **which found a second botocore logger: `botocore.regions` prints the bucket, object key and endpoint of every request it routes at DEBUG, presigns included**; pinned above DEBUG in `storage.py` beside `botocore.auth`. **Three worker riders** (found at CK-35/CK-36 verification): the `ready` line carries **`N ms claim-to-ready`** (a monotonic clock from the start of the claim to the ready commit — record §6.3's 60 s p95 measurable from ordinary logs for the first time); **`attempts` is persisted at `ready`** (`1` after a clean run, `2` after a retry or an abandoned claim) and the log prints the row's value — the two surfaces agree; **`available_at` is NULL on every terminal rung** — `ready` and every dead-letter path (the permanent failure, the third transient failure, the third abandonment at reclaim). No verifier assertion for the riders: one would fail on the four deployed rows written before the change without anything being wrong (the two CK-36 dead-letters keep their `available_at`; the two CK-36 `ready` rows their `attempts = 0`). **321 backend tests** (10 new: `tests/test_media_reads.py` 8 — a pending photograph visible to its uploader and the host and 404 to a keeper and to an accepted invitee who are neither, first; `test_worker.py` +2 — the `ready` line and terminal rows; four existing `attempts` pins moved from 0/1 to 1/2 by the rider); frontend untouched. Dev-deploy: the audience rule against `47474f49-…`/`bb21cbe4-…` as uploader/host and as a non-uploading member, the first repeatable claim-to-ready figure, the verifier at 0017/130; **(cs) separately, after 20:51 UTC 2026-09-09** (WORKING-ON-NOW checks (dd)–(dg)). Report: `D:\projects\temp\CK-37-report.md`.
- **1.31.0** (2026-09-09): Phase CK-36 — **processing a photograph: EXIF stripped, three layers written, and the publish that never lands half-done** (fifth phase of the media arc; **backend only**; **no migration** — head stays 0017, `alembic check` clean). **Step 0, HEIC settled**: CK-34's allowlist admits `image/heic` and its refusal copy names HEIC, and Pillow cannot open HEIC without `pillow-heif` — so the iPhone default, the launch shape's primary case, would have passed the intent and dead-lettered; **`pillow-heif==1.7.0` added beside `Pillow==12.3.0`**, both justified in `requirements.txt` in the webauthn/botocore voice (codec work is never hand-rolled; the wheels bundle libheif, Render's build unchanged), and `processing.DECODABLE_FORMATS` (`JPEG|PNG|WEBP|HEIF`) is the allowlist's real-bytes counterpart — pinned that every admitted format decodes; the allowlist and the decoder may never disagree. New **`app/services/processing.py`** (pure functions over bytes — no DB, no network, no logging): open and read the **header only** so the **~50 MP guard** runs before any pixel is decoded (`MAX_PIXELS`, mirrored into Pillow's own bomb limit; a 20 KB PNG of 50.4 MP is refused, exactly 50 MP passes); the real format from the bytes (a renamed `.mov`, GIF/BMP/TIFF, random bytes, a truncated JPEG — all `Undecodable`, all **permanent**); **`ImageOps.exif_transpose` FIRST, then strip** (strip-then-serve without transposing rotates every phone portrait sideways and looks like a UI bug); **strip everything by never carrying it** — every rendition re-encoded from pixels with no `exif=`/`icc_profile=`/`xmp=` handed to the encoder and the decoded image's `info` cleared first (an EXIF block "minus GPS" is a copied block); colour kept in **pixel space** (an embedded ICC profile converted to sRGB with littlecms and then not embedded; a garbage profile ignored); the three layers per media-layers §1/§4 — **archival** ≤4000 px JPEG q90 on **`STANDARD_IA`**, **web** ≤2560 px WebP q82 on `STANDARD`, **thumbnail** ≤400 px WebP down a quality ladder to ≤30 KB — **never upscaled**, three rows regardless, the storage class recorded on the row **verbatim as R2 spells it**. **The test that matters most has a real subject**: `tests/fixtures/media/` holds a genuine JPEG with a genuine GPS IFD (a public landmark) and orientation 6 with an asymmetric pixel layout, plus a PNG and a HEIC carrying the same IFD (`make_fixtures.py` beside them records provenance); the tests first assert the fixture HAS GPS, then process it and inspect all three layers two independent ways (Pillow's EXIF reader and a raw-bytes scan) — **nothing mocked**; orientation pinned pixel by pixel (the red quadrant lands top-right; no orientation tag survives to be applied twice). **`ingest.py` — the publish transaction** (record §8's word for the worker's `ready` step; the host's publication is untouched): (1) read the original (missing → permanent; a GET 404 after the HEAD found it → the same; store silent → transient; credential rejected → released); (2) decode/transpose/strip/render (`Undecodable` → **dead-letter at attempt 1**, `last_error` in operator terms); (3) write all three objects under **`storage.published_key(id, layer)`** — `media/<id>/<layer>`, a pure function, so the writes are **idempotent by construction** (a retry overwrites its own keys, never orphans a second set); (4) **ONE transaction**: the guarded `ready` update FIRST (a claim lost to a reclaim writes nothing at all), the media id's existing derivative rows **replaced** (delete-then-insert — the `uq_media_derivatives_media_id_layer` retry path can never surface as an IntegrityError dead-letter that reads as a decode bug), **`gatherings.total_bytes` incremented by the three layers' ACTUAL stored sum** in one UPDATE; (5) **only after that commit the original is deleted** — `worker._delete_original`, a boolean, never an error for the row; a crash between 4 and 5 costs a lingering original the 24-hour rule collects, the other order would cost the photograph — **pinned from a second session at the instant of the delete** (`ready`, three rows, bytes moved). **The reservation's baton verified, not rebuilt**: `account_usage` reads the declared size before and the derivatives' actual sum after — the two differ on purpose (over-counted in the safe direction). **Deleting an absent object is a SUCCESS** (`storage.delete_quarantine_object` swallows 404/NoSuchKey; R2 answers 204 anyway) because CK-35's (cw) **measured ~61 s of two live workers per deploy** — `SKIP LOCKED` protects the row, not a delete a slow-dying predecessor issues; a delete that fails outright leaves the row `ready` with a warning. **The dead-letter's object deletion lands too** (record §6.2's "at that moment" — after the `failed` commit, on every dead-letter path incl. the third abandonment at reclaim). `publication_state` stays `pending` on every path. **CK-35's release scaffolding deleted**: `NO_PROCESSOR_RECHECK` and `Outcome.RELEASED` gone, `Outcome.READY` in their place — pinned by absence. **Rider — `hide_input_in_errors=True`** on both `SettingsConfigDict`s (pydantic's missing-field error rendered `input_value=<the raw dict>` into deploy logs; CK-33's already printed 22 hex characters of a live credential's tail; `SecretStr` would not have helped) — pinned: a fake secret present and a field missing → the secret, the host and the literal `input_value` absent from the rendered error, the field still named; **gated on the prod cutover**. **Rider found in the suite: `alembic/env.py`'s `fileConfig` disabled every already-imported app logger** for the whole session (the migration runs from a session fixture after the app modules import), so no caplog assertion could see an app log line — `disable_existing_loggers=False` now; CK-34's never-log pins are the stronger for it. `verify_schema.py` → head **0017, 130 assertions** (3 new: every `ready` photograph has exactly its three layers; archival on `STANDARD_IA`, web/thumbnail on `STANDARD`; every gathering's `total_bytes` equals the sum over its ready photographs' layers — each negative-tested with scratch rows in the test DB, exit 1, restored). **310 backend tests** (46 new: `tests/test_processing.py` 22; `test_worker.py` +13 net — the release test replaced by the publish suite, the deletes-nothing pin inverted, the any-path pin narrowed to failure paths; `test_storage.py` +11, the `botocore.auth` pin among them); frontend untouched. **Dev-deploy — the first real photograph end to end**: intent → PUT → confirm with a GPS-bearing test image, three derivative rows, three objects in `coveykeep-dev`, nothing left in quarantine for that id, `ready`/`pending`, `total_bytes` non-zero for the first time, **the web derivative downloaded and confirmed GPS-free**; `processing` observable for the first time; the two evidence rows dead-letter or process (either is a pass); the verifier at 0017/130 from the Web Shell (WORKING-ON-NOW checks (cy)–(dc)). Report: `D:\projects\temp\CK-36-report.md`.
- **1.30.0** (2026-09-08): Phase CK-35 — **the worker exists, claims, and fails correctly** (fourth phase of the media arc; **the worker's skeleton only — no image processing**: Pillow, EXIF stripping, the derivative layers and the atomic `ready` transaction are CK-36; **backend only**; **no migration** — head stays 0017, `alembic check` clean). **`render.yaml` gains a second service, `covey-keep-worker` (`type: worker`, same repo/branch/database, starter plan, `python -m app.worker`) with NO `preDeployCommand` — only the web service runs `alembic upgrade head`** (pipeline record §8: two `alembic upgrade` processes racing one database fail on the first deploy after the worker exists; a comment sits beside the line most likely to be "helpfully" added); its env is **six variables, exactly** — `DATABASE_URL`, `R2_ENDPOINT_URL`, the two bucket names, `R2_WORKER_ACCESS_KEY_ID` / `R2_WORKER_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY` — and **`R2_WORKER_*` must never be added to `covey-keep-api`** (read-write on both buckets; the verifier script cannot see it in the wrong service). **The config split first, because it blocked the phase**: every web setting is required with no default and `app.config` constructed `settings` at import, so a worker importing the module for its own class could not boot without `SESSION_SECRET` and both web credentials. New **`WorkerSettings`** in `config.py` (six fields; **no field for the upload or serve credential or the session secret**; the web `Settings` class untouched and gaining no worker field — pinned on the field lists) and **the web singleton made lazy** (a module `__getattr__`; every web consumer imports `settings` at its own import, so uvicorn and alembic still fail at boot exactly as before — pinned in a subprocess); **the worker boots in a subprocess with exactly six variables set**, the web `Settings` never constructed and `app.db` never imported (pinned). `storage.py`: **`WorkerClient`**, the third type (both buckets; `worker_client` takes `WorkerSettings` and refuses the web `Settings`; refused by both presign functions — the worker has no caller to authorise; `head`/`read`/`delete_quarantine_object` accept it), and the two web factories resolve the web settings lazily on call. New **`app/services/ingest.py`** (the claim service — testable alone): claim by `SELECT … FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED LIMIT 1` over `uploaded` with `available_at` NULL or past OR `processing` stalled 15 minutes, oldest `created_at` first, stamping `claimed_at` and moving to `processing` — **`pending_upload` never claimable**; **reclaim re-claims (never delete-and-recreate) and COUNTS the abandoned attempt** — a row that keeps killing the worker must exhaust its ladder, and three abandonments dead-letter at reclaim; retry `attempts` += 1 with `available_at` pushed 1m then 5m, the third failure terminal (the 15m rung defined, unreached at three attempts); **a missing quarantine object is PERMANENT and dead-letters on the FIRST attempt** (`attempts` = 1, never 3 — the case the two deployed orphans are in); **a third class the record did not name — a rejected credential (`AccessDenied`, `InvalidAccessKeyId`, `SignatureDoesNotMatch`…) — releases the row untouched and backs the loop off** (as transient it would dead-letter every queued photograph within minutes of a dashboard typo); outcomes written with a guarded update (`WHERE status='processing' AND claimed_at=<this claim>`) so a reclaimed row's late outcome is void; `last_error` in operator terms — an exception class and an S3 code at most, never a URL, a key or a key id. **Dead-lettering releases the reservation through the one quota path** (`failed` is outside `keeping.IN_FLIGHT_STATUSES`; pinned — usage drops by the row's size; no second mechanism). **A present object is RELEASED unprocessed** — back to `uploaded`, `attempts` untouched, `claimed_at` cleared, `available_at` an hour forward (`NO_PROCESSOR_RECHECK`) so no hot loop — **scaffolding with a stated expiry: CK-36 deletes the branch**; not left `processing` (the reclaim rescues it forever), not dead-lettered (a healthy photograph destroyed). New **`app/worker.py`** (the entry point): `WorkerSettings()` → engine → `WorkerClient` → the loop — claim, settle, drain while work remains, sleep 5s jittered when idle; **never crash-loops** — a poll failing on a missing table/column or an unreachable database is logged and retried (pinned against a REAL renamed column in the test DB, then restored); SIGTERM sets the stop flag; `--once` drains and exits (0 idle / 1 not ready). **The worker deletes nothing** — `ingest.py` imports no delete or read (pinned); the dead-letter's object deletion lands with CK-36. `verify_schema.py` → head **0017, 127 assertions** (2 new: `claimed_at` set iff `processing`; every `failed` row has a `last_error` — negative-tested with scratch rows in the test DB, 2 FAILs + exit 1, restored). **264 backend tests** (23 new in `tests/test_worker.py`; prior tests unedited); frontend untouched; `.env.example` says where the worker's two credential halves go (the shell, never the file). **Dev-deploy — the deliverable is the two orphaned rows**: `926ed780-…` and `806981d9-…` dead-letter on the first attempt each, the reservation releases by 4096 bytes, `5371a749-…` is untouched, neither service fails on a migration race, the worker survives a redeploy with nothing stranded, the verifier reads 0017/127 with `media rows: 3` (WORKING-ON-NOW checks (ct)–(cx)).
